## Current continuation

Work is ongoing on the exact TU2/Xenia DLC reference and the native runtime path. The next verification gates are Xenia input delivery and a clean native rebuild with the Legends Pack entitlement proven in-game; no completion claim is made yet.

This project is an active engineering build for a fan made Fight Night Champion recompilation and browser viewing experience. Work is moving forward, but it is not a finished game release.

## Gameplay playability

The game opens and reaches playable boxing in the current local test runtime, with boxing interaction and HUD progression demonstrated. The normal launch path still needs its logging-path repair before it is the standard one-click route. The project remains a playable-game effort, but a complete bout, save/reload coverage, and a distributable build remain open.

## Working now

- The native runtime scaffold builds successfully in debug and release configurations.
- Native lifecycle, foreign function interface, and stereo bridge checks pass.
- The local launcher and browser entry points bundle successfully.
- The launcher signaling test suite passes when run with its isolated test ports.
- Source and evidence are organized for continued decompilation and renderer work.

- Thirty bounded decompilation batches have completed with independent review: 948 callers plus seven retained controls were captured in paired baseline and candidate runs. The newest batch is retained as raw representation evidence only; cumulative semantic coverage remains unchanged and behavior is not claimed.

## Being investigated

- Completing the full game decompilation and proving that reconstructed code matches the original behavior.
- Diagnosing why some custom fighters and outfit pieces can appear without their intended textures.
- Connecting renderer resource tracing to a verified left and right eye presentation path.
- The normal one-click launcher still has a separate logging-path defect (`0xC0000409`) in its rotating log sink. That is not a game startup or playability failure: the writable local test runtime launched successfully, reached an active fight, and was cleanly shut down. The private receipt records that successful playable run; only the launcher logging repair and texture trace remain open.

## Not proven yet

The following acceptance gates remain open:

- A complete, behaviorally equivalent decompilation.
- A complete playable game build with saved progress and a full bout.
- Correct custom character and outfit textures in all relevant scenes.
- Independent left and right eye rendering, verified parallax, and a playable stereo experience.
- Standalone browser execution without a separately running local game host.
- A self contained downloadable or installable game package from this repository.

The repository does not contain the commercial game image or other proprietary game files. It cannot download or install those files. Anyone testing the project must provide their own legally obtained game inputs and keep them outside version control.

## Decoder652 launch-path diagnosis — 2026-10-24

unner.py intentionally requires the OS-reported parent image to equal the pinned controller C:\Users\samsa\.cache\codex-runtimes\codex-primary-runtime\dependencies\native\powershell\pwsh.exe. launch.ps1 starts the Hermes Python child directly, which is valid only when the packet itself is invoked by that exact pinned pwsh. The prior decoder652 failure therefore came from the outer launch host, not a decoder semantic result. ## Local readback-mode gap identified — 2026-10-27

Source inspection found a concrete Reborn/Venom divergence in `research/xenia-source/src/xenia/gpu/command_processor.cc`: `ReadbackResolveMode` has only disabled, fast, and full. Any unrecognized string, including `smart`, falls through to `kFast`; the D3D12 path then reads the previous buffer and has no steady-result skip mode. Reborn documents `smart` plus `readback_resolve_smart_skip_steady=true` for GPU-built created-fighter layers. The finding is recorded in `research/local-readback-smart-gap-20261027.json`. This is a real source/runtime gap relevant to the black-skin and presentation issues, but it is not yet proven to be the sole skin cause; a reviewed runtime implementation and a fresh Legacy training comparison are still required.

## Reborn texture comparison — 2026-10-27

- Reborn’s documented fix path uses `readback_resolve=smart`, a per-address repeated-resolve tracker, and immediate copies for non-steady resolves; steady resolves may be delayed or skipped. It specifically covers clothing and created-fighter skin layers.
- Venom’s pinned command processor currently accepts only `none`, `fast`, `some`, and `full`; the active candidates pass `smart` but the parser normalizes it to disabled. This explains why the candidate flag did not activate the Reborn behavior. A source-only smart-readback candidate is being prepared in an isolated folder.
- Existing evidence still shows full readback helping clothing while custom skin remains black, so this comparison identifies a concrete missing mechanism but does not yet prove the skin fix.
- Luna prepared `research/active-work/readback-smart-candidate/` from the pinned Venom command processor. The isolated parser/CVAR diff is hash-pinned and preserves the `none` default, but it is source-only and does not yet include the companion enum/D3D12 tracker needed for a build. No texture acceptance is claimed.
